Accepted + pending checkpoint + residue equals the current denominator. Pending intake remains residue, not accepted credit.

Residual bands are reported, not remeasured; unknowns are never guessed. The 0 (held) band includes held and pending-intake exact bodies. Blocker labels marked [INFERENCE] are keyword summaries; exact-blocker pointers are authoritative.

Only a structured documented_residue user decision naming the function and an existing ruling Markdown resolves a structured user-ruling hold; prose never grants this class. It does not mark missing ladder rungs completed. Optional disposition evidence never changes a class. Revisit witnesses add provenance only, not objective rung completion.

Actionable incomplete-ladder/intake functions: **0**. Required search is rungs 1–3 plus dispositioned rung 4 (frozen 32 B bound); 5/5b/5c are extras. Full ladder, hold, census mapping and hash detail: `RESIDUE-r17.json`.
